- **baton**: Baton: save a resume prompt for whatever this session is doing, and release the plan if one is in-session. - **bbc-analyze**: BBC — design the black boxes of a new project, or of a new part of one, from the requirements, with the user, before any code ({@tasks}/bbc/): the black boxes by responsibility, their contracts, dependencies and flows, the decisions of the architecture. No code is read. The diagram is approved before the details; then /dev:bbc-review, and back here with its findings until the user approves the map. Use when a project, or a part of it, too large for one context window is to be built as black boxes, or the user says bbc analyze, mappa BBC, black box map, scomponi in BB, progetta a black box. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/bbc-analyze.md))
- **bbc-audit**: BBC — check whether the code reflects the analysis: each black box's code in its namespace, its contract in the Core as the map writes it, the dependencies only through the Core and only those on the map, the decisions of the map kept. It writes a report, and the findings are fixed by one feature. Use when the roadmap reaches the audit, after BBs were built or changed, or the user says bbc audit, il codice rispecchia la mappa, controlla il codice contro la BBC. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/bbc-audit.md))
- **bbc-core**: BBC — plan the Core of the contracts, the first step of the roadmap; on a project with no code yet it is the project's start — the profile, the skeleton, then the Core. Every contract of the map written in the project's Core — the abstract concepts of the whole system, each BB's interfaces and the types they exchange under the BB's namespace — with its fake and its contract tests, so that every BB can then be developed in parallel against it. A TDDAB plan, reviewed, then it STOPS and the user triggers /dev:j-cvm-exec-plan. Use when the roadmap's first step is due, or the user says bbc core, s… ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/bbc-core.md))
- **bbc-debug**: BBC — fix a bug that crosses several black boxes. The bug path of /dev:j-bug — LISTEN, DIAGNOSE with Protocol D, PLAN — with ISOLATE done on the map, the BBs' tests run leaves first, then the integration points; then STOPS, the user triggers /dev:j-cvm-exec-plan. Use when a failure spans several BBs and cannot be placed, or the user says bbc debug. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/bbc-debug.md))
- **bbc-e2e**: BBC — plan the end-to-end tests of the map's flows: every flow that crosses the black boxes, through the real transport and the real storage, at the level the project has (the API, the user interface). A TDDAB plan, reviewed and ready for /dev:j-cvm-exec-plan. Use when the user says bbc e2e, test dei flussi, or /dev:bbc-integrate pointed here. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/bbc-e2e.md))
- **bbc-help**: Look up quick reference information for Acorn BBC Micro (Model A, B, B+, Master 128) 6502 development. Read and use the files under `reference/` to answer questions about: ([reyco2000/6502-dissasembler-skill](https://github.com/reyco2000/6502-dissasembler-skill/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/bbc-help.md) · ★1)
- **bbc-integrate**: BBC — integrate black boxes after one of them changed its contract or they were built apart. Every call between them is checked on both sides of the contract, and a TDDAB plan proves them wired together, real BB with real BB; the map's integration points are updated. Use when the roadmap says built BBs meet, after /dev:bbc-plan changed a contract, or when the user says bbc integrate, integra i BB, test tra i BB. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/bbc-integrate.md))
- **bbc-plan**: BBC — plan the development of one black box as a feature. From the BB's definition in the map — its responsibility, its contract, its dependencies — the phases of /dev:j-new-feature scoped to the BB write a TDDAB plan, the cases of the contract as the tests and the implementation behind it, the other BBs as the fakes of the Core, the Core's contract tests run against the real implementation; the plan is reviewed, then it STOPS and the user triggers /dev:j-cvm-exec-plan. Use when the user says bbc plan, sviluppa il BB, pianifica il BB, or names a black box of the roadmap to build. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/bbc-plan.md))
- **bbc-refactor**: BBC — change a black box of the map — its responsibility, its contract, a split or a merge, a decision — and then the code that follows it: the phases of a feature up to a reviewed plan, the map changed and approved first. Use when the user says bbc refactor, cambia il BB, sposta la responsabilità, dividi o unisci i BB, or /dev:bbc-audit sent a finding of the map here. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/bbc-refactor.md))
- **bbc-review**: BBC — review the analysis: the black-box map against the requirements and the method — the boundaries, the contracts, the connections, the logic of the flows, the requirements and the decisions — with no code read; write what is wrong with where and why, and send it back to /dev:bbc-analyze, until the user approves the map; then propose the roadmap of the development — the Core of the contracts, the BBs (in parallel when the user wants), the integrations, the flows, the audit of the code — approved by the user and written in the map. Use after /dev:bbc-analyze, before working on the BBs, or w… ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/bbc-review.md))
- **bbc-work**: BBC — delegate the work on one black box to its own git worktree and agent session. It opens the worktree and its branch, writes there the BB's scope and the Memory Bank, and STOPS with the command that opens a session in that folder; there the BB is developed with /dev:bbc-plan, and closed with /dev:j-close. Several BBs can be delegated at the same time. Use when the user says bbc work, lavora sul BB, delega il BB, or a step of the roadmap sends BBs in parallel. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/bbc-work.md))

- **bec-init**: 识别最近工程和已有规范，先确认任务边界与语言证据，再提出最小初始化；未经明确要求不下载编译器、不替换现有构建文件、不生成框架应用。 ([bovinphang/backend-craft](https://github.com/bovinphang/backend-craft/blob/HEAD/localized/zh-CN/commands/bec-init.md) · ★1)
- **bec-init**: Inspect the nearest project and existing repository standards. Confirm the task boundary and language evidence before proposing minimal setup. Never download a compiler, replace existing build files or initialize a framework without an explicit request. ([bovinphang/backend-craft](https://github.com/bovinphang/backend-craft/blob/HEAD/commands/bec-init.md) · ★1)

- **bec-plan**: 阅读相关模块和约束，比较方案，选择最小完整设计，按顺序写出任务、失败处理和可观察的验收检查。 ([bovinphang/backend-craft](https://github.com/bovinphang/backend-craft/blob/HEAD/localized/zh-CN/commands/bec-plan.md) · ★1)
- **bec-refactor**: 明确不变量与调用方，建立安全网，每次执行一个保持行为的变换，并验证错误、顺序、资源与持久化语义。 ([bovinphang/backend-craft](https://github.com/bovinphang/backend-craft/blob/HEAD/localized/zh-CN/commands/bec-refactor.md) · ★1)
- **bec-refactor**: Identify the invariant and affected callers, establish a safety net, and perform one behavior-preserving transformation at a time. Verify errors, ordering, resources and persistence after every meaningful step. ([bovinphang/backend-craft](https://github.com/bovinphang/backend-craft/blob/HEAD/commands/bec-refactor.md) · ★1)

- **bec-review**: 只读评审指定变更及调用方，报告可执行的问题、级别、位置、触发条件与建议回归，并说明验证缺口。 ([bovinphang/backend-craft](https://github.com/bovinphang/backend-craft/blob/HEAD/localized/zh-CN/commands/bec-review.md) · ★1)
- **bec-tdd**: 用聚焦回归描述一个可观察行为，实际运行并确认预期失败，再最小实现，重跑用例和相关套件；不把跳过当通过。 ([bovinphang/backend-craft](https://github.com/bovinphang/backend-craft/blob/HEAD/localized/zh-CN/commands/bec-tdd.md) · ★1)

Script-based `PPDMDatabaseCreatorService` runs apply to **packaged PPDM script trees** and ([The-Tech-Idea/Beep.OilandGas](https://github.com/The-Tech-Idea/Beep.OilandGas/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/beep-dataaccess-database-creation.md))

- **beep-dataaccess-generic-repository**: The `PPDMGenericRepository` is a non-generic repository implementation that provides CRUD operations for any PPDM entity without requiring specific repository classes for every table. It uses metadata to automatically determine table names, primary keys, and relationships. ([The-Tech-Idea/Beep.OilandGas](https://github.com/The-Tech-Idea/Beep.OilandGas/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/beep-dataaccess-generic-repository.md))
- **beep-dataaccess-index**: **Feature extension tables:** entity classes + **`IModuleSetup.EntityTypes`** + **`CreateSchemaFromEntitiesAsync`** / migration — **not** new hand-written `Beep.OilandGas.Models/Scripts/**`. See root **`CLAUDE.md`** (*Schema for extension tables*). [Database Creation](beep-dataaccess-database-creation.md) and [Script Execution](beep-dataaccess-script-execution.md) describe **tooling** over script ([The-Tech-Idea/Beep.OilandGas](https://github.com/The-Tech-Idea/Beep.OilandGas/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/beep-dataaccess-index.md))
- **beep-dataaccess-lov-management**: The `LOVManagementService` provides CRUD operations and querying capabilities for the `LIST_OF_VALUE` table, managing List of Values (LOV) data used throughout PPDM39. - **beep-dataaccess-mapping**: The `PPDMMappingService` provides centralized conversion between DTOs and PPDM model classes. - **beep-dataaccess-metadata**: The metadata management system provides access to PPDM39 table metadata, including table definitions, column information, relationships, and subject area organization. - **beep-dataaccess-overview**: The `Beep.OilandGas.PPDM39.DataManagement` project provides a comprehensive data access framework for working with PPDM39 (Petroleum Public Data Model version 3.9) databases. It offers metadata-driven, database-agnostic data access patterns that work with any data source through the Beep Data Management Engine. ([The-Tech-Idea/Beep.OilandGas](https://github.com/The-Tech-Idea/Beep.OilandGas/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/beep-dataaccess-overview.md))
- **beep-dataaccess-patterns**: This document outlines common patterns and best practices for using the PPDM39 Data Access Framework. ([The-Tech-Idea/Beep.OilandGas](https://github.com/The-Tech-Idea/Beep.OilandGas/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/beep-dataaccess-patterns.md))
- **beep-dataaccess-quality**: The data quality services provide comprehensive data quality assessment and monitoring capabilities. - **beep-dataaccess-script-execution**: These services **execute** scripts from a configured directory (discovery, ordering, engine). See **`CLAUDE.md`** and [Database Creation](beep-dataaccess-database-creation.md). ([The-Tech-Idea/Beep.OilandGas](https://github.com/The-Tech-Idea/Beep.OilandGas/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/beep-dataaccess-script-execution.md))
- **beep-dataaccess-seed-data**: The seed data management system provides comprehensive capabilities for seeding PPDM39 databases with reference data, LOV data, and initial data sets from CSV files and JSON templates. - **beep-dataaccess-validation**: The `PPDMDataValidationService` validates PPDM entities against business rules and data quality rules. - **beep-dataaccess-versioning**: The `PPDMDataVersioningService` tracks entity versions and changes over time, providing versioning capabilities for audit and rollback operations.
